HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_18c0dda8090e244c54b6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:30:3\",\"root_001520\",[\"B003\"]],[\"32:30:5\",\"root_001520\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bekleme kökünün yüz yüze durma çağrışımı, bedensel ayrılığa rağmen iki tarafı aynı gelecek olay çevresinde zamansal bir karşılaşma içinde tutuyor olabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_19fcf98b2914b6b1cbb1","text":"No later ayah follows to reactivate this section. Its surprise is retrospective in the reverse direction: the repeated **ن ظ ر** closes the surah by converting the denied future respite of 32:29 into a present, explicitly temporary interval of mutual waiting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_742bc17d50822fd52675","text":"2. **Waiting keeps opponents mutually oriented (exploratory).** **ن ظ ر B003** supplies facing so that parties remain visible to one another, while the reciprocal repetition انتظر / منتظرون creates formal symmetry. The explicit physical verb says turn aside; I therefore do not erase separation. I infer instead that shared orientation toward one future event keeps the separated parties in a temporal face-off. They are apart in posture but still confronted by the same awaited disclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001001/B005","root_001520/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_74f7bcf2fe22c1c85711","text":"1. **Mirrored withdrawal and bounded suspension.** **ع ر ض B005** supplies turning one's side away; **ن ظ ر B002** supplies waiting for the time and granting delay. Their earlier turning from signs in 32:22 is now answered by a command using the same side-turning root, while both sides are described as waiting. I infer that withdrawal suspends fruitless engagement without resolving the dispute by force. The command reads as a temporary relational consequence bounded by the opening day of 32:29.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001001/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8b6667ec080e1c860ae1","text":"1. **The earlier turn-away is answered by a commanded withdrawal of engagement.** `ع ر ض:B005` is turning one's side away. In 32:22 the offenders performed *aʿraḍa ʿanhā* after reminder; here the addressee receives *fa-aʿriḍ ʿanhum*. **Inferred arrow:** repeated turning from signs -> argumentative engagement reaches its limit -> the messenger is released from pursuing their response. The mirrored construction is reciprocal in form but not in cause: their turn blocks guidance, while this turn follows completed warning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9f1582ddbe48b18aa5ae","text":"None; this is the packet's final ayah, so no later ayah was available for retrospective activation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c3c9874b3fe919024965","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:30:3\",\"root_001520\",[\"B002\"]],[\"32:30:5\",\"root_001520\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aynı bekleme kökü iki tarafı tek geleceğe bağlasa da beklentilerini, zaman üzerindeki denetimlerini ve sonuçlarını eşitlemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001520/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c4ce0460e011da7b2387","text":"2. **The same waiting root holds two unequal postures.** `ن ظ ر:B002` covers awaiting an appointed time: the imperative *intaẓir* and participle *muntaẓirūn* place both sides in relation to the same future. Yet 32:29 used the same branch in passive form to deny them respite at the opening. **Inferred arrow:** shared waiting interval -> asymmetrical expectations -> one event resolves both. Lexical symmetry does not imply equal control over timing or equal outcome.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001001/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c5d61e078e8fbfbecc9a","text":"3. **Withdrawal is paired with vigilance, not indifference.** `ع ر ض:B005` redirects the addressee away from the people, while `ن ظ ر:B002` immediately commands continued watch for the time. The coordinated imperatives prevent either root from operating alone. **Inferred arrow:** cease fruitless confrontation + remain temporally attentive -> let the announced event, rather than further dispute, perform verification. The surah closes its speech contest without abandoning orientation to the result.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001001/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e2f3c3d5b991e5c4bb3e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:30:1\",\"root_001001\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İşaretlerden sürekli yüz çevirenlere karşı aynı fiille geri çekilme emri verilmesi, tartışmayı sürdürme yükünün sona erdiği ilişkisel bir sonuçtur; iki dönüş biçimce benzer, gerekçede farklıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
